// ====================================================================
// Production Experiment Batch File
// Based on experiment plan from commit 1e31a00
// Path: StraightLine + 90Turn
// ====================================================================
//
// Structure:
//   8 scenarios x 13 algorithms x 30 trials = 3120 total trials
//
// Scenarios:
//   1. Scenario 1: StraightLine, Single User, No Obstacles
//   2. Scenario 2: StraightLine, Two Users, No Obstacles
//   3. Scenario 3: StraightLine, Single User, 4 Obstacles
//   4. Scenario 4: StraightLine, Two Users, 4 Obstacles
//   5. Scenario 5: 90Turn, Single User, No Obstacles
//   6. Scenario 6: 90Turn, Two Users, No Obstacles
//   7. Scenario 7: 90Turn, Single User, 4 Obstacles
//   8. Scenario 8: 90Turn, Two Users, 4 Obstacles
//
// Algorithms (13 total):
//   1. PredRedLPP_Default
//   2. PredRedLPP_H1
//   3. PredRedLPP_H5
//   4. PredRedLPP_MinAction
//   5. PredRedLPP_E7
//   6. PredRedLPP_E15
//   7. PredRedLPP_MaxComp
//   8. PredRedLPP_H1_MinAction
//   9. PredRedLPP_H5_MinAction
//   10. PredRedLPP_E15_MinAction
//   11. ThomasAPF
//   12. S2C
//   13. Null
//
// ====================================================================
